## page_0531_human

THE RULE [CANON]
OF THE EXALTATION OF THE HOLY CROSS

On the Sunday of the Exaltation of the Holy Cross. In the night there is a vigil; they shall place the Holy Relics upon the cushions in the chancel. Processional hymn, Tone 7 [dzy / dzk]: "The Power of the Holy Cross" (Zorutyun surb khachi). And the priests shall vest together and come into the chancel, and they shall begin the morning hymn, Tone 1 [ah / ay]: "He who [stretched out] your spotless arms" (Or zanarat bazuks ko), with its canons. 
Morning song: "You have given a sign to those who fear you."
Litany: "Let us say."
"Holy God," [who was] crucified [for us]. 
And they shall go forth with the Cross, the Gospel, and all adornments in procession, saying the hymn Tone 1 [ay]: "He who [stretched out] your spotless arms" (Or zanarat bazuks).
Psalm 4: "There was signed" [Show us the light of your countenance]; Response: "When I called."
Proverbs 14:1 "Again, if [one preparing to] sail..." to verse 8: "...the one who made it."
Prophet Ezekiel 9:2 "And behold, six men..." to verse 6: "...do not come near."
Apostle 1 Corinthians 1:18 "For the preaching of the cross..." to verse 24: "...[the power of God and the] wisdom [of God]."
Alleluia: "There was signed."
Gospel Matthew 24:27 "For as the lightning..." to verse 35: "...shall not pass away."
Litany: "For the peace from above."
And they shall bless the four corners of the world [Andastan].
Afterwards, Litany: "By your Holy Cross."
Prayer: "Protect us."
And then: "Newly created" (Norasteghtsyal).
Litany: "Let us beseech," "Save [us]."
Prayer: "To your almighty."
And then they shall place the Holy Cross and the Gospel upon the stand, and the priests and the congregation shall bow down together, saying the hymn Tone 2 [bk]: "Before your Cross, O Christ, we bow down, and your holy burial we magnify, and your holy resurrection we glorify," etc.
Likewise: "Glory to your holy Cross, Alleluia; to your crucifixion, Alleluia; to your holy resurrection, Alleluia," etc., as at Easter.
Afterwards, Litany: "By your Holy Cross."
Prayer: "Protect us."

At the Midday Liturgy, Introit: "Before the honorable."
Response: "The Lord reigns."
Hymn Tone 6 [gy / gzk]: "Victorious" and "A new blessing."
Psalm 4: "There was signed."
Prophet Isaiah 49:13 "Be glad [O heavens]..." to verse 23: "...they waited for me."
Apostle Galatians 6:14 "But God forbid that I [should glory]..." to verse 18: "...brethren, Amen."
Alleluia: "Give power."
Gospel John 3:13 "And no man hath ascended..." to verse 21: "...they are wrought [in God]."
Hymn of the Hagiology: "Who is like [unto our God]."

But before the evening hour, they shall place the Holy Cross, which contains a portion of the True Wood, on a silver platter in the vestry; and they shall adorn it with basil and rosewater, and they shall cover it with a precious cloth.

And at the ninth hour, they shall assemble in the church, and the Bishop shall vest along with the priests and deacons, and they shall go with censers, lanterns, and liturgical fans to the Holy Cross. And two senior priests shall lift up the Holy Cross on the platter.

## page_0532_auto

...highest. And the clergy shall minister round about. And the bishop, after arriving with the censer in hand, shall come in procession before the main altar; and they shall begin the hymn, Tone 3 [gi]: "Who dawned upon us." And before the altar, the bishop shall say: "Lord our God, Who didst establish in heaven the hosts and armies of angels," etc. See the Liturgy Book. And in worship they shall place the Holy Cross upon the altar, and they shall offer incense, saying:
Psalm 96 [97]: "Exalt [the Lord]."
The Apostle, 1 Corinthians 1:18: "For the preaching of the cross," to verse 24: "wisdom."
Alleluia: "A sign was given."
Gospel, John 12:23: "Father, glorify," to verse 33: "He was to die."
And they shall take the Holy Cross from the altar in the same order, and with the priests elevating it, they shall go toward the western side, saying: "Holy God," three times. "Holy and mighty," likewise. "Holy and immortal," likewise. After which: "Who wast crucified for us, have mercy on us."
And they shall begin the hymn, Tone 6 [gki]: "Today the appearing."
After which, [the deacon says]: "Proschume" [Orthi].
Gospel, John 3:13: "And no one has ascended into heaven," to verse 21: "were done."
And the deacon shall proclaim in a loud voice:
"For our holy Patriarch [Name], and for all orthodox bishops, priests, and deacons, and for all the children of the covenant of this holy church, let us pray to the Lord."
And the bishop shall say "Lord have mercy" forty times.
The deacon shall say: "Bless, Lord."
And the bishop, taking the Holy Cross from the priests and elevating it, shall sign it with the sign of the cross, saying:
"May the Eastern side of the world, and the Patriarchate of the Armenians, and all orthodox bishops and priests, together with deacons and clerks, and all our people, men and women, elders and children, and those who are gathered in faith in this holy church, and our city and provinces, and all who dwell in them in orthodoxy, be blessed, protected, and preserved by providence; by this sign of the holy cross, and by this holy gospel, and by the grace of this day, in the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, now and always and unto the ages of ages. Amen. Alleluia."
And they shall place the Holy Cross on the paten and go to the eastern side, saying: "Holy God," three times. "Holy and mighty," likewise. "Holy and immortal," likewise. After which: "Who wast crucified for us, have mercy on us."
And then the hymn, Tone 1 [adz]: "Who Thine immaculate arms."
After which: "Proschume" [Orthi].
Gospel, Matthew 24:27: "For as the lightning," to verse 35: "shall not pass away."
And the deacon shall proclaim:
"For the peace of the whole world, and for devout Christian kings, and for all their people who believe in Christ, let us pray to the Lord."
And the bishop shall say "Lord have mercy" forty times.
And the deacon shall say: "Bless, Lord."
And the bishop, taking the Holy Cross and elevating it, shall say:
"May the Western side of the world, and the kingdom, the land, and all Christian people, be blessed, protected, and preserved by providence; by this sign of the holy cross and by this holy gospel and by the grace of this day, in the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, now and always and unto the ages of ages. Amen. Alleluia."
And having placed the Holy Cross on the paten, they shall go toward the northern side, saying: "Holy God," three times, as written above.
And then the hymn, Tone 3 [gi]: "Wood of life."
After which: "Proschume" [Orthi].
Gospel, Mark 15:20: "And they led Him out," to verse 26: "King of the Jews."
And the deacon shall proclaim:
"For the fields, vineyards, and other fruitful lands of this country, let us pray to the Lord."
And the bishop shall say "Lord have mercy" forty times.
And the deacon shall say: "Bless, Lord."
And the bishop, taking the Holy Cross from the paten and elevating it, shall sign it with the sign of the cross, saying:
"May the Southern side of the world, this land, fields, vineyards, [and all fruitful places, be blessed, protected, and preserved by providence...]"

[Note on liturgical variations:]
When there are eight weeks in this interval, you shall celebrate the feasts of this week in order as they are set down.
But if there are seven weeks, celebrate the feast of Severianus along with the Hyperichians.
And on Thursday, celebrate John Chrysostom.
And on Saturday, celebrate the feast of All Saints without fail, as the Calendar shows you.

Know, O lover of feasts, that if the period of eating meat is seven weeks, this Tenth Sunday is always the Eve [Barikendan] of the Advent Fast. The days of the feasts of the Annunciation and of the Ascension.
The Saturday of the Discovery of the Relics of the Holy Enlightener.
The Monday of Vardavar [the Transfiguration], of the Assumption of the Mother of God, and of the Exaltation of the Holy Cross.
The days of the three feasts of the Holy Mother of God: that is, the Conception, the Nativity, and the Presentation.
The Saturday of the first Enlighteners, Thaddeus and Bartholomew.

But in various places, they are also accustomed to keep Sunday-rest on the Saturday of the feast of the Archangels, on the feast day of Thaddeus and Sandukht, on the days of the Entrance and Exit of the holy Enlightener into the Pit [Khor Virap], and on the days of the feasts of the Vardanants and of the Holy Translators.
[Number.] Gospel according to Luke 2:8: "And there were shepherds..." to verse 14: "...good will toward men." Litany: "By this Holy Cross." Prayer: "Guardian [Pahpanya]." Our Father who art in heaven.
And glory to Christ forever and ever, Amen.
Instruction concerning the keeping of Sunday-rest on certain days.
Apart from the natural Sundays, the other days to be kept as Sunday-rest are these:
The second and eighth days of the Nativity and Theophany.
The days of the Presentation of the Lord to the Temple [Tyarnndaraj] and of Great [Holy] Thursday.

## page_0555_human

A BRIEF PREAMBLE
CONCERNING THE SECOND VOLUME OF THIS TONATSUYTS [DIRECTORY]
... [untranslatable string of index letters]

First, let it be known that:
This Second Volume was created as a rule and guide for the First Volume.
Because those intervals of time which increase and decrease, and whose specific details the First Volume cannot display,
as well as the feasts that fall within such intervals and, being movable, cannot be celebrated in the same place [every year]—
this Second Volume displays these, regulated in all details, as you can see.
But those intervals and feasts which are immovable, and which are set down in the First Volume, always remain unchanged in the same way;
this Second Volume does not mention or display them, but instructs you to look in the First Volume in each respective place, as you can see.
Yet, within these intervals, certain feasts that are sometimes relocated due to the requirement of the Calendar Letter, and do not remain the same,
this Second Volume lists by name and mentions.
Therefore, do not be confused by such things; for whatever this Second Volume mentions,
know that these things are altered in that year.
It also mentions the [hymns of the] Fathers [Harts], the days of the month, and the daily tones, so that you may know them.
And whatever it leaves out and does not mention, you must look up in the First Volume and proceed according to the order.
For this reason, it is written in many places in this Second Volume: "See Volume 1," preserving the order of the place,
so that you may know that you must always keep the table of the Calendar Letter in hand alongside this Second Volume,
and proceed according to its indications with the First Volume.

Second: This Second Volume instructs you to look for certain intervals of some calendar letters under other letters,
as you can see under B, C, E, and so on.
The reason for this is that if we had put everything under every letter, there would have been much repetition, and the Tonatsuyts book would have grown too large.
Therefore, just as we directed you to look in the First Volume for the immovable feasts,
likewise, concerning certain movable intervals and feasts,
if they were found to be identical in form under some letters,
we placed them in full under one calendar letter in this Second Volume,
and under the other matching letters, we wrote to look under that letter, in which there is no difficulty.

Third: In this Second Volume, under some letters, we have written brief instructions regarding leap years.
You must observe those instructions only in that leap year in which those same letters are the calendar letters, and not in other years.
For example, under the calendar letter T, it is written in small cursive script:
"When a leap year occurs, and the letter of the year is 19, on this Saturday do not celebrate [Saint] Theodosius," etc.
You must observe this instruction only in that year which is a leap year and when the letter of the year is 19.
But in other years, whether leap year or not, celebrate without hesitation just as is set down under each respective letter.
Keep this same rule for all letters where such instructions are written concerning the leap letters.

Fourth: At the beginning of whichever table the daily tones are not placed,
you must maintain the order of the tones from the day of the Eve of Theophany [Birth],
and the [hymns of the] Fathers [Harts] of the Martyrs and of the Fasts, and the Ascension [Hambartsi] hymns of the Resurrection,
up to the Sunday of Real Good Friday [Great Lent Eve].
For it was not possible to place them at the beginning of every letter.

## page_0556_human

These [are ordered] because they do not succeed each other in the same manner year after year.

Fifth: From the first Sunday after the Nativity until the Sunday of the Eve of mid-Fast [Median Autumn / Mid-Lent], however many Sundays may occur, for their evening Resurrection Hampartsi [hymns of Ascension/Dismissal] — of whatever tone they may be — you must say the "Ter Herknits" [Praise the Lord from the heavens] unto the very end, and conclude [thus] in every yearly calendar. But on other Sundays where it is written "to the end," there also you must say it to the end; and where "to the end" is not placed, you must say only that which is placed.

Sixth: The feasts of the Saints for the most part are set down plurally in our Tonatsuyts [Directory] from its original state, that is, two, three, four, five, and others, many or few, which are always celebrated together indivisibly, as you see. These we have likewise placed in the First Volume as before. But when we mention such in this Second Volume, we have placed only the name of the first saints in many places for the sake of brevity; nevertheless, when you encounter such feasts in this Second Volume, you must celebrate, along with the first-named saint, all the other companions following him, and commemorate their names by that canon as they are set down in the First Volume.

Seventh: In our Tonatsuyts, the feasts which frequently shift and are celebrated in various and different intervals are these:

The feasts written after the Nativity with penance;

The feasts after the second week of Vardavar [Transfiguration];

The last feasts of the fourth week of the Assumption of the Mother of God, namely: Andrew the General and Adrian; and the very last two feasts, namely: Abraham and Khoren, and Astuatsatur [Adeodatus]. Now, I have mentioned these for this reason, so that you may mark them and know their places, so that wherever you may celebrate them, you shall see their exemplars in their proper place.

Monday. Tone 5 [gz]. Fast. Feast of the Conception of the Holy Virgin from Anna.

Sunday. Tone 2 [ak]. Fifth [Sunday]. Canticle of the Fathers [Harts] Tone 6 [gk]: "Pre-eternal" (Nakhayavitean). Resurrection Hymn at Evening [Hambartsi] Tone 2 [ak]: "Risen" (Yarutsyal), and "Wonderful" (Sqancheli), and "Wondrous" (Hrashali).
Monday. Tone 3 [bz]. David the Prophet, and James the Apostle.
Sunday. Tone 1 [ahz/adz]. Sixth [Sunday]. Forefeast (Barekendan) of the Fast of the Nativity. Canticle of the Fathers [Harts] Tone 5 [g3]: "Only-begotten Son." Resurrection Hymn at Evening [Hambartsi] Tone 1 [adz]: "Awesome sight" (Sokali tesil), to the end.

25. Saturday [Shb]. Tone 4, Plagal [Dk / D2]. Anton, and Tryphon, Barsamas and Onuphrius the hermits. If the year-letter is 8 [Te / Թ] in a leap year, on this Saturday do not celebrate Anton, because it was celebrated under the first letter 9 [To / Թ] after the Theophany [Birth of Christ]; but on Saturday celebrate Theodosius; on Monday, Kyriakos; on Tuesday, Vahan; and on Thursday, Tryphon with their companions. But in ordinary years, as it is set, celebrate in this manner.

6. Sunday. Tone 3 [GDz]. Third [Sunday]. Canticle of the Fathers [Harts] Tone 2 [B]: "Who art the King" (Որ թագաւորդ ես). Lord I Have Cried Hymn [Hambartsi] Tone 3 [GDz]: "Glorified" (Փառաւորեալ). Barekendan [Eve] of the Fast of Saint James.

9. Wednesday. Tone 8 [DK]. Feast of the Conception of the Holy Virgin by Anna.

21. Monday. Tone 4 [BK]. Saints Abraham and Khoren, and Saints Cosmas and Damian. Canticle of the Fathers [Harts] Tone 8 [OK]: "Holy God" (Սուրբ աստուած).

5. Tuesday. Tone 3 [BDz]. Eve of the Nativity of Christ our God.

8. Sunday. Tone 1 [ADz]. Ninth [Sunday]. Lord I Have Cried Hymn [Hambartsi] Tone 8 [ODz]: "With the women" (Ընդ կանայսն), and "Who Ineffable" (Որ անճառ).

15. Sunday. Tone 8 [DK]. Tenth [Sunday]. Lord I Have Cried Hymn [Hambartsi] Tone 8 [DK]: "Thy angel, O Lord" (Հրեշտակ քո տէր), to the end. Barekendan [Eve] of the Fast of the Advent [Yisnakats].

21. Saturday. Tone 6 [GK]. Feast of the Presentation of the Holy Mother of God.

5. Saturday. Tone 4 [BK]. Saints Nicholas, and Gregory the Wonderworker, Patriarchs; and the other Patriarch Nicholas, and Bishop Myron. Magnificat [Mankunk] on the note: "They wondered" (Հիացան). Morning hymn, Tone 3 [BDz]: "Who with light" (Որ լուսով).

THE SEVEN-DAY CYCLE OF THE ROMANS IN JANUARY:

January:

6. MONDAY. Feast of the Nativity and Epiphany of Christ. The duration of non-fasting days [Utis] is fourteen days.

14. Tuesday. Saint John the Forerunner [Precursor].

16. Thursday. Saint Peter the Patriarch, etc.

18. Saturday. Saint Anthony the Hermit.

19. Sunday. Canticle of the Fathers [Harts] Tone 7 [DDz]: "Dawn of the Only-begotten" (Ծագումն միածնի). Barekendan [Eve] of the Fast of the Catechumens [Arachavorats].

26. Sunday. Saint Sarkis. Canticle of the Fathers [Harts] Tone 5 [GDz]: "Glorifying" (Փառաւորելով). And see the others in order in the First Volume up to Transfiguration [Vardavar], keeping the order of the place.

February:

9. Sunday. Tone 8 [DK]. Real Barekendan [Quinquagesima Sunday].

14. Friday. Tone 5 [GDz]. Presentation of the Lord [Tyarnndarach].

Saturday. Tone 6 [GK]. Of the Holy Patriarchs: Athanasius and Cyril.

Sunday. Tone 1 [AH]. Fifth [Sunday after Pentecost]. Canticle Father [Harts] Tone 6 [Gz]: Glorified God. [Hambartsi] Tone 2 [Bk]: Anticipating [the women].

Monday. Of Eugenios, Macarius, Valerius, Candidus, and Aquilas. Canticle Father [Harts] Tone 8 [Dk]: Who with miraculous power.

Saturday. Of the Holy Council of Ephesus, of the two hundred Patriarchs.

Sunday. Tone 2 [BK]. Sixth [Sunday after Pentecost]. Canticle Father [Harts] Tone 4 [Dg]: We glorify You. [Hambartsi] Tone 6 [Ez]: The divine [tomb]. Fast-breaking [Barikendan] of the Fast of the Holy Mother of God.

Monday. Fast of the Holy Mother of God. Canticle Father [Harts] of Fasting Tone 1 [Ah] (or Tone 2 [Ap]): Who awesome.

Sunday. Assumption of the Holy Mother of God. The intervening meat-eating period is three weeks. See in the First Volume, keeping the order of this place.

...

September

Sunday. Tone 2 [AK]. Fourth [Sunday after the Assumption]. Canticle Father [Harts] Tone 6 [Gk]: Ineffable. [Hambartsi] Tone 7 [Dz]: The risen Lord. Fast-breaking [Barikendan] of the Fast of the Holy Cross.

Monday. Tone 3 [BDz]. Fast. Feast of the Nativity of the Holy Mother of God.

Tuesday. Tone 4 [BK]. Of the Fast. Canticle Father [Harts] of Fasting Tone 4 [Bk]: [We raise] the voice of the youths.

Sunday. Tone 1 [ADz]. Exaltation of the Holy Cross. See the 14th Sunday in the First Volume, and keep the order for the feast and the fast. And for the Epistles and the daily lectionary readings and the calendar cycle, keep the order.

...

November

Sunday. Tone 8 [DK]. Tenth [Sunday]. Fast-breaking [Barikendan] of the Fifty-day Fast [Yisnakats]. [Hambartsi] Tone 6 [Gk]: Your angel, O Lord, to the end.

23. Tuesday. Tone 3 [GD]. [Feast of] David the Prophet and James the Apostle.

January

4. Sunday. Tone 1 [AD]. Seventh [Sunday], Fast. Second Canticle [Hambartsi], Tone 1 [AY]: "Awesome vision" (Sokali tesil), to the end.

The Roman Cycle of Seven, 2.

January

6. Tuesday [AVURAKE]. Feast of the Nativity and Epiphany of Christ. The period of eating meat [non-fasting days] is fifteen days.

14. Monday. [Feast of] John the Forerunner.

15. Tuesday. [Feast of] Patriarch Peter, Vlas [Blaise], and the others.

17. Thursday. [Feast of] Anton [Anthony] the Anchorite.

19. Saturday. [Feast of] Theodosius the King and the Holy Infants of Ephesus.

1. Thursday. Tone 4 [BK/b2]. [Feast of] Tryphon, Parsamas, and Onuphrius the hermits.

THE SEVEN-YEAR CYCLE OF THE ROMANS 1.
In January:

6. SATURDAY. Feast of the Nativity and Theophany of Christ. The fast-free period is fifteen days.
13. Saturday. Eighth day of the Nativity, and the Circumcision of Christ.
14. Sunday. First [Sunday]. Tone 3 [gd]: [Hymn for the] Father of the Only-begotten.
15. Monday. [Feast of] John the Forerunner.
16. Tuesday. [Feast of] Peter the Patriarch, Blaise, and others.
18. Thursday. [Feast of] Anthony, and Tryphon, Barsama, and Onuphrius the hermits.
20. Saturday. [Feast of] Theodosius the King, and the Holy Infants of Ephesus.
21. Sunday. Tone 7 [dz]. [Hymn for the] Father: Glorified. Barekendan [Eve] of the Fast of the Catechumens. And for the rest, see the First Volume up to Transfiguration [Vardavar], keeping the order of this place.

In February:
11. Sunday. Tone 8 [dk]. Great Barekendan [Eve of Great Lent].
14. Wednesday. Tone 4 [bz]. Presentation of the Lord [Tyarnndaraj]. See according to the order in the First Volume.

In April:
1. Sunday. Tone 1 [az]. Easter: Day of the Resurrection of Christ.
7. Saturday. Tone 7 [dz]. Feast of the Annunciation of the Holy Virgin Mary.

In May:
20. Sunday. Tone 2 [ak]. Coming of the Holy Spirit [Pentecost]. Barekendan of the Fast of Elijah.
27. Sunday. Tone 1 [az]. First [Sunday]. Beginning of the Sundays of the Resurrection. Tone 1 [adz]. [Hymn for the] Father: Who came for salvation.

In July:
8. Sunday. Tone 3 [bz]. Transfiguration [Vardavar]. The fast-free period is four weeks. See up to the Friday of the third week in the other volume; and on Saturday come here, and celebrate these feasts set down here, which are after the eight days of the Nativity. But if it is a leap year, and the letter of the year is 30 [L-I], celebrate here only [Gregory] the Theologian, because the other patriarchs were in the first letter 30 [L]. But in other years, as they are set down here, so shall you celebrate.

1. Monday. Tone 8 [DK]. [Feast of] Gennarius and Mercurius, and of Jacob and Irava-P'har [Just-Glorious].

2. Tuesday. Tone 1 [ATS]. [Feast of] Cornelius the Centurion, and of Simeon, and the others.

7. Sunday. Tone 6 [GK]. Third [Sunday of Advent]. Canticle of the Fathers (Harts) Tone 5 [bz]: "Who art the King" (Or t'agavord es). Lord's Prayer/Evening Hymn (Hambartsi) Tone 6 [gk]: "Taking and our" (Areal ev mer). Fast-break (Barekendan) of the Fast of Saint James.

9. Tuesday. Tone 8 [DK]. Fast. Feast of the Conception of the Holy Virgin by Anna.

10. Wednesday. Tone 1 [ATS]. Fast. Canticle of the Fathers (Harts) Tone 8 [dk]: "God of our fathers, have mercy" (Astvats harts'n merots oghormya).

14. Sunday. Tone 5 [GZ]. Fourth [Sunday of Advent]. Canticle of the Fathers (Harts) Tone 4 [bk]: "Unoriginate God" (Anskizbn astvats). Lord's Prayer/Evening Hymn (Hambartsi) Tone 5 [gz]: "Glorified is thy resurrection" (P'aravoryal e qo yarut'iund).

21. Sunday. Tone 4 [BK]. Fifth [Sunday of Advent]. Canticle of the Fathers (Harts) Tone 5 [gz]: "Glorified Son of God" (P'aravoryal ordi astutso). Lord's Prayer/Evening Hymn (Hambartsi) Tone 4 [bk]: "Christ is risen" (K'ristos yarev), and "Morning" (Aravotyan), and "They anticipated" (Kankhets'in).

22. Monday. Tone 5 [GZ]. [Feast of] Abraham and Khoren, Cosmas and Damian, and of Astvatsatur [Theodotos] the Martyr. Canticle of the Fathers (Harts) Tone 8 [dk]: "To thee, God" (Ar qez astvats).

23. Tuesday. Tone 6 [GK]. [Feast of] David the Prophet and James the Apostle.

28. Sunday. Tone 3 [BZ]. Sixth [Sunday of Advent]. Canticle of the Fathers (Harts) Tone 6 [gk]: "Before eternity" (Nakhahavityan). Lord's Prayer/Evening Hymn (Hambartsi) Tone 3 [bz]: "Today they rejoiced" (Aysor zvart'ats'an).

29. Monday. Tone 4 [BK]. [Feast of the] Sons of Thunder [James and John]. Fast-break (Barekendan) of the Nativity Fast.

30. Tuesday. Tone 5 [GZ]. Fast of the Nativity of Christ. Canticle of the Fathers (Harts) Tone 6 [gk]: "Blessed Lord God, blessed" (Orhneal ter astvats orhneal). And see the others in order.

January

4. Sunday. Tone 2 [AK]. Seventh [Sunday of Advent]. Fast. Lord's Prayer/Evening Hymn (Hambartsi) Tone 2 [ak]: "Wonderful is the Resurrection" (Hrashali zyarut'iun).

5. Monday. Tone 3 [BDZ]. Eve of the Nativity of Christ.

Seven-Day Cycle of the Romans:

19. Saturday. [Feast of] Theodosius the Emperor, and the Children of Ephesus.

20. Sunday. First [Sunday after Epiphany]. Canticle of the Fathers (Harts) Tone 5 [dz]: "The dawning of the Only-Begotten" (Tsagumn miatsni).

21. Monday. [Feast of] Cyriacus and Julietta, and of Gordius, Polyeuctus, and Gregory.

22. Tuesday. [Feast of] Vahan of Goghtn, and of Eugine the Virgin, and the others.

January

6. SUNDAY. Feast of the Nativity and Epiphany of Christ. The meat-eating season (non-fasting period) is twenty-two days.

14. Monday. [Feast of] John the Forerunner [Baptist].

15. Tuesday. [Feast of] Peter the Patriarch, Blaise, and the others.

17. Thursday. [Feast of] Anton [Anthony] the Anchorite.

24. Thursday. Feast of Triphon, Barsamas, and Onuphrius the hermits.
26. Saturday. Feast of Athanasius and Cyril the Patriarchs.
27. Sunday. Second. [Tone 2] Barekendan [Pre-Lenten Eve] of the Fast of Catechumens. For the others, see the First Volume up to Vardavar [Transfiguration], keeping the order of this place.

4. Monday. Feast of the Atomian Generals, and Bishop Mark, Pion, and the others.
11. Monday. Feast of the Vardanian Generals.

17. Sunday. Tone 8 [DK / ԴԿ]. Great Barekendan [Eve of Great Lent]. See also the others according to their order in the First Volume.

7. Sunday. Tone 1 [ADz / ԱՁ]. Easter [Zatik], Day of the Resurrection of Christ, and the Feast of the Annunciation of the Holy Virgin.

June
2. Sunday. Tone 1 [ADz / ԱՁ]. First. Beginning of the Sundays of the Resurrection. Canticle [Harts] God: "Who came for salvation" (Որ եկիր ի փրկութիւն).
July
14. Sunday. Tone 3 [BDz / ԲՁ]. Vardavar [Transfiguration]. The period of non-fasting [utikh] is four weeks. See in the First Volume up to the Friday and Saturday of the third week which is here; but look here for the Sunday Canticles [Harts].
21. Sunday. Tone 2 [AK / ԱԿ]. Second. Canticle [Harts] Tone 3 [BDz / ԲՁ]: "Who descended from heaven, without beginning" (Որ ի յերկնից իջեր անսկիզբն).

August
3. Saturday. Tone 7 [DDz / ԴՁ]. Feast of Gregory the Theologian.
4. Sunday. Tone 8 [DK / ԴԿ]. Fourth. Canticle [Harts] Tone 7 [DDz / ԴՁ]: "We glorify" (Փառաւորեմք).
5. Monday. Tone 1 [ADz / ԱՁ]. Feast of Eugene [Eugenios], Macarius, and the others. Canticle [Harts] Tone 7 [DDz / ԴՁ]: "Holy art Thou, Lord of hosts" (Սուրբ ես տէր զօրութեանց). But know this: if a leap year occurs and the year-letter is Ղ, Ձ, or Չ, do not celebrate Eugene and Macarius here, because they are celebrated in the first year-letter after the Nativity, as you can see; instead of them, celebrate Abraham, Khoren, and Astvatsatur, who are placed below under the virgin Juliana. But in other years, celebrate without hesitation as it is written.
6. Wednesday. Tone 2 [AK / ԱԿ]. Feast of Andrew the General and his army, and the others.
8. Thursday. Tone 4 [BK / ԲԿ]. Feast of Adrian [Adrianos] and his wife Anatolia, and the others. Canticle [Harts] Tone 8 [DK / ԴԿ]: "To Thee, O God" (Առ քեզ աստուած).
10. Saturday. Tone 6 [GK / ԳԿ]. Feast of the holy ecumenical Council of Ephesus, the two hundred Patriarchs.
11. Sunday. Tone 7 [DDz / ԴՁ]. Fifth. Canticle [Harts] Tone 6 [GK / ԳԿ]: "Ineffable" (Անճառելի). Barekendan of the Fast of the Holy Mother of God.

18. Sunday. Tone 6 [GK / ԳԿ]. Assumption [Verapokhum] of the Holy Mother of God. The period of non-fasting [utikh] is three weeks. See this period in the First Volume, keeping the order of this place.

23. Tuesday. Tone 1 [Adz]. Fast day. Canticle of the Fathers of the Fast, Tone 4 [Bk]: "Blessed art thou of our fathers" (ԶՀարցըն մերոց օրհնեալ).

26. Saturday. Tone 4 [Bk]. Feast of Thomas the Apostle. See also the others in order in the First Volume.

2. Saturday. Tone 3 [Bz]. Feast of James and Simon the Apostles. Read the scripture and the gospel of the Tuesday of the Fast of Saint James.

3. Sunday. Tone 4 [Bk]. Fourth Sunday. Canticle of the Fathers, Tone 5 [Gz]: "We glorify thee" (Փառաւորեմք զքեզ). Hymn of the Dwelling, Tone 4 [Bk]: "Christ is risen" (Քրիստոս յարեաւ).

4. Monday. Tone 5 [Gz]. Feast of Andrew the General and his army, etc.

5. Tuesday. Tone 6 [Gk]. Feast of Adrian and his wife Anatolia, etc. Canticle of the Fathers, Tone 6 [Gk]: "To thee, O God" (Առ քեզ աստուած).

8. Friday. Tone 1 [Adz]. Feast of the Nativity of the Holy Mother of God.

9. Saturday. Tone 2 [Ak]. Feast of the Holy Council of Nicaea, of the three hundred and eighteen patriarchs.

10. Sunday. Tone 3 [Bz]. Fifth Sunday. Canticle of the Fathers, Tone 6 [Gk]: "Ineffable" (Անճառելի). Hymn of the Dwelling, Tone 3 [Bz]: "At the tomb" (Ի գերեզման) and "They came to the tomb" (Եկին ի գերեզ). Barekendan [Eve] of the Fast of the Holy Cross.

11. Monday. Tone 4 [Bk]. Fast of the Holy Cross. Canticle of the Fathers of the Fast, Tone 4 [Bk]: "Lord God of our fathers" (Տէր աստուած հարցն մերոց).

17. Sunday. Tone 2 [Ak]. Exaltation of the Holy Cross. See until the tenth Sunday in the First Volume; but for the canticle of the martyrs and of the fasts, and the resurrection hymns of the dwelling, and the tone of the days, see under the letter 'G' (Ղ) of the Directory, keeping this month.

20. Monday. Tone 2 [Ak]. Fast of the Fifty-day Fast. Canticle of the Fathers of the Fast, Tone 5 [Gz]: "Blessed art thou forever" (Օրհնեալ ես յաւիտեան).

21. Tuesday. Tone 3 [Bz]. Fast day. Feast of the Presentation of the Holy Mother of God.

26. Sunday. Tone 8 [Dk]. First Sunday. Canticle of the Fathers, Tone 2 [Ak]: "Unoriginate Word" (Անսկիղբն բանդ). Hymn of the Dwelling, Tone 8 [Dk]: "The hosts of angels" (Հրեշտակացն դասք) and "Thy angel, O Lord" (Հրեշտակ քո տէր).

27. Monday. Tone 1 [Adz]. Feast of Julian and Vasilouhi. Canticle of the Fathers, Tone 4 [Bk]: "We bless thee, Lord" (Օրհնեմք զքեզ տէր). See also the others in the First Volume, keeping the proper order of this place.

3. Sunday. Tone 7 [Dz]. Second Sunday. Canticle of the Fathers, Tone 3 [Bd]: "Who art King" (Որ թագաւորդ ես). Hymn of the Dwelling, Tone 7 [Dz]: "Who art above" (Որ ի վերդ ես).

4. Monday. Tone 8 [Dk]. Feast of Genarius, Mercurius, and Yakovik and Irava-Zmampar.

5. Tuesday. Tone 1 [Adz]. Feast of Abgar the King.

9. Saturday. Tone 5 [Gz]. Feast of the Conception of the Holy Virgin by Anna.

10. Sunday. Tone 6 [Gk]. Third Sunday. Canticle of the Fathers, Tone 4 [Bk]: "Unbegotten..."

29. Thursday. [Feast of] Adrian and his wife Natalia, and the others. But know that when it is a leap year, and the letter of the year is N3, during this period do not celebrate Andrew the Commander and Adrian, because their feast is on the final letter Y, on the tenth Sunday. Instead, on Monday celebrate only Vahan, on Tuesday Eugenia the Virgin, and on Thursday Eugenius, Macarius, and the others. But in other years, celebrate without fail exactly as written here.

ROMAN CALENDAR OF SEVEN-DAY WEEKS [Roman Directory]
January

6. Saturday. Feast of the Nativity and Theophany of Christ. The dispensation of eating meat [non-fasting period] is thirty days.
14. First Sunday. Tone 7 [dzk]: Canticle [Harts], "Rising of the Only-begotten" (Dagoomn miadzni).
15. Monday. Feast of John the Forerunner [John the Baptist].
16. Tuesday. Feast of Peter the Patriarch, Blaise, and the others.
18. Thursday. Feast of Anthony, and Triphon, Parsamas, and Onuphrius the solitary monks.
20. Saturday. Feast of Theodosius the King, and the Holy Infants of Ephesus.
21. First Tone 5 [gz]: Canticle [Harts], "Creator of all creatures" (Ararichd araradzots).
22. Monday. Feast of Cyriacus and Julitta, and of Gordius, Polyeuctus, and Grigoris.
23. Wednesday. Feast of Vahan of Goghtn, and of Eugine the Virgin, and the others.
25. Thursday. Feast of Eugenius, Macarius, and the others.
27. Saturday. Feast of the Holy Patriarchs Athanasius and Cyril, and of Gregory the Theologian.
28. Third Sunday. Tone 6 [gcl]: Canticle [Harts], "Today he was sacrificed" (Aysor zenav).
29. Monday. Feast of the Maccabees, Eleazar, and the others.
30. Tuesday. Feast of the Twelve Prophets: Hosea, and the others.

February

1. Thursday. Feast of Sophia and her three daughters.
3. Saturday. Feast of the Sons and Grandsons of the Enlightener [St. Gregory]. See these four feasts after the feast of Transfiguration [Vartavar]. But know this, that when a leap year occurs and the letter of the year is [letter code], do not celebrate these four feasts here, namely the Maccabees and the others, because they are celebrated on the last calendar date [letter code] after Transfiguration, as you see. Instead, celebrate on Monday the feast of Eugine the Virgin, on Saturday Gregory the Theologian, which were placed above with the others, and on Tuesday and Thursday Andrew the General and Arrianus, which are placed below after the Assumption of the Mother of God. But in other years, celebrate without doubt exactly as it is set down.
4. Fourth Sunday. Eve [Barigentan] of the Fast of the Catechumens [Arachavorats].
11. Sunday of Saint Sarkis.
14. Wednesday. Presentation of the Lord [Tyarnuntarach].
25. Sunday. Tone 8 [dkl]. Sunday of Great Lent [Boon Barigentan]. See also the others according to their order in the First Volume.

April

7. Saturday. Tone 1 [ahz]. The Raising of Lazarus, and the Feast of the Annunciation of the Holy Virgin.
15. Sunday. Tone 1 [az]. Easter [Zatik]: Day of the Resurrection of Christ. See according to the order in the First Volume.

May

24. Thursday. Tone 8 [dkl]. The Ascension of Christ. See according to the order in the First Volume.

3. Sunday. Tone 2 [akl]. The Coming of the Holy Spirit [Pentecost]. Eve [Barigentan] of the Elijah Fast.

Sunday. Tone 1 [adz - Ayb-Dza]. Third [Sunday]. Feast of the Holy Cross of Varag. The post-feast period of eating meat is seven weeks.

25. Sunday. Tone 2 [AK]. Third [Sunday after the Cross]. Feast of the Cross of Varag. The intervening period of eating meat [non-fasting period] is eight weeks.

2. Sunday. Tone 1 [ACH]. Fourth [Sunday after the Cross]. Hymn of the Evening [Hambartsi] Tone 1 [A3]: "With the women" (Ynd kanaysn).
9. Sunday. Tone 8 [DK]. Fifth [Sunday after the Cross]. Hymn of the Evening [Hambartsi] Tone 8 [DK]: "When they came" (Yorzham yekin), and "The angel descended" (Ej hreshtakn), and "The ranks of angels" (Hreshtakats'n dasq).

30. Sunday. Tone 5 [GZ]. Eighth [Sunday after the Cross]. Hymn of the Evening [Hambartsi] Tone 5 [G3]: "By the wood of the sign of the cross" (I khachanish payten).

19. Saturday. Tone 1 [ACHZ]. All Saints, old and new, known and unknown.
20. Sunday. Tone 2 [AK]. Eleventh [Sunday after the Cross]. Canticle of the Fathers [Harst] Tone 1 [AD]: "Blessed art thou, Lord" (Orhneal es ter). Hymn of the Evening [Hambartsi] Tone 2 [AK]: "He is risen" (Yaruts'eal) and "Wondrous" (Sqanch'eli). Barekendan [eve] of the Fast of the Fifty Days [Advent].
21. Monday. Tone 3 [BDZ]. Fast. Feast of the Presentation of the Holy Mother of God.

But know this, that when a leap year occurs, and the year-letter is [R], during this interval you shall not celebrate the feasts of Andrew the commander and Adrian, because they are celebrated in the last year-letter [R], after the tenth Sunday, as you see. Instead, on Monday celebrate Vahan, on Tuesday Eugenia the virgin, and on Thursday Eugenius. But in other years, celebrate them without doubt as they are set down here.

THE SEVEN-YEAR CYCLE OF THE ROMANS 1.
However, when it is a leap year, and
the year-letter is 208 [ԸծՏ], do not celebrate
these four feasts, namely: Cyprian, Athanagenes,
the Forefathers, and Thaddeus the Apostle,
at this time. For their feasts are celebrated
in the last year-letter, 300 [Տ], after Transfiguration,
as you see. But in place of Cyprian, celebrate Eugene the Virgin,
and in place of Thaddeus, [celebrate] Gregory the
Theologian, who was placed above with the other
feasts; and in place of Athanagenes
and the Forefathers, celebrate Andrew the Commander
and Adrian, who
are placed below after the Mother of God.
But in other years, as it is written,
so celebrate without doubt.

and the rest. Father of Tone 1 [ԲՁ]: "Und
soorpus ko" [Among Your saints].

25. Sunday, Tone 1 [aկ/ԱԿ]. Mid-Lent [Michink]. Dismissal Hymn [Hambartsi] Tone 1 [aկ]: "We shall sing to You" (Yerkestsook kez). And you shall celebrate the rest in order as in the First Volume, keeping the order of the day.

March

4. Sunday, Tone 8 [dկ/ԴԿ]. Great Barekendan [Boon Barekendan / Eve of Great Lent].
7. Saturday, Tone 3 [gձ/ԳՁ]. Saint Cyril of Jerusalem, etc. — and of the Patriarchs: John of Jerusalem, John of Odzun [Otsnetsi], etc.
24. Saturday, Tone 6 [bկ/ԲԿ]. Saint Gregory our Enlightener's entry into the Pit [Virap].

April

7. Saturday, Tone 1 [aկ/ԱԿ]. Feast of the Annunciation of the Holy Virgin.
22. Sunday, Tone 1 [aձ/ԱՁ]. Easter [Zatik], the Day of the Resurrection of Christ. See also the others according to their order in the First Volume.

May

31. Thursday, Tone 8 [dկ/ԴԿ]. Ascension of Christ. And the rest according to the order.

June

10. Sunday, Tone 1 [aկ/ԱԿ]. Pentecost (Coming of the Holy Spirit). Day of abstinence for the Fast of Elijah.
17. Sunday, Tone 1 [aձ/ԱՁ]. First [Sunday]. Beginning of the Sundays of the Resurrection. See the Canticles of the Fathers [Harts] of the Sundays here.

July

8. Sunday, Tone 7 [gկ/ԳԿ]. Fourth [Sunday]. Canticle of the Fathers [Harts] Tone 1 [aձ]: "Who came for salvation" (Vor yekir i prkutyun).
Sunday, Tone 6 [bկ/ԲԿ]. Sixth [Sunday]. Canticle of the Fathers [Harts] Tone 2 [bձ]: "Who descended from heaven" (Vor i yernkits ijer). Day of abstinence for the Fast of Vardavar [Transfiguration].
Sunday, Tone 2 [bձ/ԲՁ]. Vardavar [Feast of the Transfiguration]. The period of non-fasting is one week. And see the rest according to the order.

August

Saturday, Tone 1 [aձ/ԱՁ]. The Holy Ecumenical Council of Ephesus of the two hundred Patriarchs.
Sunday, Tone 1 [aկ/ԱԿ]. Second [Sunday]. Canticle of the Fathers [Harts] Tone 2 [bձ]: "Who in the heavenly hosts" (Vor yerknayin zinvorutyantsd). Day of abstinence for the Fast of the Holy Mother of God [Asdvadzatsin].
Monday, Tone 2 [bձ/ԲՁ]. Fast day. Canticle of the Fathers [Harts] of Penitence, Tone 1 [aկ]: "Glorified forever" (Paravoryal yavedyan).
Sunday, Tone 1 [aձ/ԱՁ]. Assumption [Verapokhum] of the Holy Mother of God. The period of non-fasting is four weeks. See according to the order in the First Volume up to the fourth Sunday, keeping the order of the day.

September

Sunday, Tone 7 [gկ/ԳԿ]. Fourth [Sunday]. Canticle of the Fathers [Harts] Tone 2 [bձ]: "We glorify You" (Paravoremp zkyez). Dismissal Hymn [Hambartsi] Tone 7 [gկ]: "The Divine" (Zastvadsayin).
Monday, Tone 3 [gձ/ԳՁ]. Saint Andrew the Commander and his army, etc.
Tuesday, Tone 8 [dկ/ԴԿ]. Saint Adrian [Hadrian] and his wife Anatolia, etc. Canticle of the Fathers [Harts] Tone 6 [bկ]: "Those in battle" (Vork i mardi).
Wednesday, Tone 1 [aձ/ԱՁ]. Canticle of the Fathers [Harts] of Penitence, Tone 6 [bկ]: "Blessed art thou, Lord, God of our fathers" (Hartsən merots orhnyal es der).
Thursday, Tone 1 [aկ/ԱԿ]. Saint Abraham and Saint Khoren, Saint Cosmas and Saint Damian, and Saint Asdvadzadoor [Adeodatus] the Witness. Canticle of the Fathers [Harts] Tone 2 [bձ]: "Who the torments" (Vork ztanjans).
Saturday, Tone 6 [bկ/ԲԿ]. Feast of the Nativity of the Holy Mother of God from Anna.
Sunday, Tone 3 [gձ/ԳՁ]. Fifth [Sunday]. Canticle of the Fathers [Harts] Tone 7 [gկ]: "Ineffable" (Anchareli). Dismissal Hymn [Hambartsi] Tone 3 [gձ]: "The oil-bearing women" (Yughaber) and "Today the Resurrection" (Aysor yarutyun). Day of abstinence for the Holy Cross.
Monday, Tone 7 [gկ/ԳԿ]. Fast of the Holy Cross. Canticle of the Fathers [Harts] Tone 6 [bկ]: "God of our fathers" (Vor hartsən merots astvads).
Sunday, Tone 6 [bկ/ԲԿ]. Exaltation of the Holy Cross [Khachverats]. See up to the tenth Sunday in the First Volume, keeping the order of the day.

23. Sunday. Tone 2 [badz, ԲՁ]. Second [Sunday of Cross]. Resurrection Night Office Hymn Tone 2 [badz, բձ]: "To the Tomb" (I gerezman) and "They Came to the Tomb" (Yekin i gerezman). Eve of the Fast of the Cross of Varag.
24. Monday. Tone 2 [bekg, ԲԿ]. Fast. Saint Mamas, etc. Fathers Hymn Tone 8 [dadz, դձ]: "We Who Hope" (Yousatsyalqn).
26. Wednesday. Tone 3 [gadz, ԳԿ]. Fast. Fathers Hymn Tone 5 [gadz, գձ]: "Glorified Is Your Name Forever" (Paravoryal anoun ko yavedyan), O Lord God.
30. Sunday. Tone 1 [ahg, ԱԿ]. Third [Sunday of Cross]. Feast of the Cross of Varag. The meat-eating period is seven weeks.
October:
7. Sunday. Tone 1 [ahf, ԱՁ]. Fourth [Sunday of Cross]. Resurrection Night Office Hymn Tone 1 [ahf, ած]: "Glory to the Resurrection" (Parg haroutyan) and "Along with" (Und ka).
14. Sunday. Tone 4 [dadg, ԴԿ]. Fifth [Sunday of Cross]. Resurrection Night Office Hymn Tone 4 [dadg, դկ]: "When They Came" (Yorzham yekin) and "The Angel Descended" (Ej hreshtakn) and "The Choir of Angels" (Hreshtakatsn dasq).
21. Sunday. Tone 4 [dadz, ԴԶ / ԴՁ]. Sixth [Sunday of Cross]. Resurrection Night Office Hymn Tone 4 [dadz, դձ]: "To the Women" (Kanantsn) and "With an Awesome Voice" (Ahagin dzayniv).
28. Sunday. Tone 3 [gadz, ԳԿ]. Seventh [Sunday of Cross]. Discovery of the Holy Cross.
30. Tuesday. Tone 4 [dadg, ԴԿ]. Saints Hyperichius and Severian, etc. Fathers Hymn Tone 5 [gadz, գձ]: "You Who the Torments of the Lawless" (Ork ztanjans anorinats).
31. Wednesday. Tone 1 [ahf, ԱՁ]. Fathers Hymn Tone 5 [gadz, գձ]: "Blessed Are You, O Lord" (Orhnyal es ter). For the Covenant. And see the rest according to the order.
November:
3. Saturday. Tone 2 [bekg, ԲԿ]. All Saints, old and new, etc.
4. Sunday. Tone 3 [gadz, ԳՁ]. Eighth [Sunday of Cross]. Resurrection Night Office Hymn Tone 3 [gadz, գձ]: "By the Sign of the Cross" (Aykhachanishn / I khachanishn).
11. Sunday. Tone 2 [bekg, ԲԿ]. Ninth [Sunday of Cross]. Resurrection Night Office Hymn Tone 2 [bekg, բկ]: "Christ Is Risen" (Kristos haryav) to the end.
18. Sunday. Tone 2 [badz, ԲՁ]. Tenth [Sunday of Cross]. Resurrection Night Office Hymn Tone 2 [badz, բձ]: "That Evangelizing Voice" (Zayn avedavor) to the end. Eve of the Fast of the Fifty Days [Advent].
19. Monday. Tone 2 [bekg, ԲԿ]. Fast of the Fifty Days [Advent]. Fathers Hymn Tone 5 [gadz, գձ]: "God of Our Fathers Who Saved" (Hartsn merots astuats or prgetser).
21. Wednesday. Tone 3 [gadz, ԳԿ]. Fast. Feast of the Presentation of the Holy Mother of God.
25. Sunday. Tone 1 [ahg, ԱԿ]. First [Sunday of Advent]. Fathers Hymn Tone 2 [badz, բձ]: "Incorruptible" (Anabagan). Resurrection Night Office Hymn Tone 1 [ahg, ակ]: "Risen" (Haroubyal) and "The Wonderful Angel" (Sqancheli hreshtakn).
26. Monday. Tone 2 [badz, ԲՁ]. Saints Julian and Vasilouhi. Fathers Hymn Tone 1 [ahg, ակ]: "Holy God, Creator" (Sourb astuats ararich).
December:
2. Sunday. Tone 1 [ahf, ԱՁ]. Second [Sunday of Advent]. Fathers Hymn Tone 2 [bekg, բկ]: "Without Beginning, God" (Ansgizbn astuats). Resurrection Night Office Hymn Tone 1 [ahf, ած]: "Who by Ineffable Essence" (Or anchar eutyamp).
3. Monday. Tone 1 [ahg, ԱԿ]. Saints Gennarius and Mercurius, and James and Rhypasimus [Iravapar].
4. Tuesday. Tone 2 [badz, ԲՁ]. King Abgar. Lectionary Readings and Gospel of Theodosius.
9. Sunday. Tone 4 [dadg, ԴԿ]. Third [Sunday of Advent]. Feast of the Conception of the Holy Virgin by Anna.
12. Wednesday. Tone 2 [badz, ԲՁ]. Fathers Hymn Tone 4 [dadz, դձ]: "Lord God of" (Ter astuats mer).
16. Sunday. Tone 4 [dadz, ԴՁ]. Fourth [Sunday of Advent]. Fathers Hymn Tone 5 [gadz, գձ]: "Glorified Son" (Paravoryal ordi). Resurrection Night Office Hymn Tone 4 [dadz, դձ]: "You Who Are Above" (Or i ver es).
22. Saturday. Tone 3 [gadz, ԳՁ]. Holy Patriarchs Basil, etc.
23. Sunday. Tone 3 [gadz, ԳԿ]. Fifth [Sunday of Advent]. Fathers Hymn Tone 3 [gadz, գկ]: "Pre-eternal" (Nakhayavedyan). Resurrection Night Office Hymn Tone 3 [gadz, գկ]: "Your Resurrection" (Zko yaroutyund) and "Taking" (Aryal).
24. Monday. Tone 4 [dadz, ԴՁ]. David the Prophet and James the Apostle.
29. Saturday. Tone 2 [bekg, ԲԿ]. Sons of Thunder [Saints James and John]. Eve of the Nativity Fast.
30. Sunday. Tone 3 [gadz, ԳՁ]. Sixth [Sunday of Advent]. Fast of the Nativity of Christ. Resurrection Night Office Hymn Tone 3 [gadz, գձ]: "Glorified Is Your" (Paravoryal e ko) to the end.
31. Monday. Tone 4 [dadg, ԴԿ]. Fast. Fathers Hymn of Fasting Tone 4 [dadg, դկ]: "Blessed Are You, Lord God of Our Fathers" (Orhnyal ter astuats hartsn merots), and so forth in order.
January:
5. Saturday. Tone 2 [badz, ԲՁ]. Eve of the Nativity of Christ [Theophany].

THE SEVEN-WEEK CYCLE OF THE ROMANS [Roman Calendar]

July

30. Monday. Tone 4 [Dkh / ԴՁ]. Cyprian the Bishop, and the others. Canticle of the Fathers [Harst] Tone 2 [Bk / բկ]: "We bless" (Orhnemk).

31. Tuesday. Tone 4 [Dk / ԴԿ]. Athenogenes the Bishop, and the others. Canticle of the Fathers [Harst] Tone 2 [Bk / բկ]: "Who in the conflict" (Ork i marti).

August

1. Wednesday. Tone 1 [Ah / ԱՁ]. Canticle of the Fathers of Repentance [Harst aghts.] Tone 2 [Bk / բկ]: "Who didst save" (Or apreguts'er).

2. Thursday. Tone 1 [Ak / ԱԿ]. The Holy Forefathers: Adam, Abel, and the others.

4. Saturday. Tone 2 [Bk / ԲԿ]. The sons and grandsons of the Enlightener [St. Gregory].

5. Sunday. Tone 3 [Gz / ԳՁ]. Fourth [Sunday]. Canticle of the Fathers [Harst] Tone 3 [Gk / գկ]: "Today He was sacrificed" (Aysor zenao). Dismissal Hymn [Hambartsi] Tone 3 [Gz / գձ]: "To the voice" (I dzayn).

6. Monday. Tone 3 [Gk / ԳԿ]. The Maccabees, Eleazar, and the others. Canticle of the Fathers [Harst] Tone 3 [Gz / գձ]: "Who the torments" (Ork ztanjanatsn).

7. Tuesday. Tone 4 [Dz / ԴՁ]. The Twelve Prophets: Hosea, and the others.

8. Wednesday. Tone 4 [Dk / ԴԿ]. Canticle of the Fathers of Repentance [Harst aghts.] Tone 3 [Gz / բձ]: "Who art awesome" (Or ahavor).

9. Thursday. Tone 1 [Ah / ԱՁ]. Sophia and her three daughters. Canticle of the Fathers [Harst] Tone 4 [Dk / դկ]: "Those who hoped" (Husats'ealkn).

11. Saturday. Tone 2 [Dz / ԲՁ]. Thaddeus the Apostle and Sandukht the Virgin.

12. Sunday. Tone 2 [Bk / ԲԿ]. Fifth [Sunday]. Dismissal Hymn [Hambartsi] Tone 2 [Bk / բկ]: "The Resurrection of Christ" (Zharut'iun K'ristosi). Fast-Breaking [Barekendan] of the Catechumens [Arachavorats].

13. Monday. Tone 3 [Gz / ԳՁ]. Fast of the Catechumens [Arachavorats]. Canticle of the Fathers of Repentance [Harst aghts.] Tone 2 [Bk / բկ]: "Blessed art Thou, Lord, God of our fathers" (Harts'n merots' orhneal es Ter).

14. Tuesday. Tone 3 [Gk / ԳԿ]. Of the Fast. Presentation of the Lord [Tearndaraj].

March

5. Sunday. Tone 4 [Dk / ԴԿ]. Great Fast-Breaking [Bun Barekendan]. See also the others according to their order in the First Volume, following the order of this place.

January

6. Friday. Tone 4 [Dz / ԴՁ]. Feast of the Nativity and Theophany of Christ. The interval of eating [non-fasting days] is thirty-eight days.

14. Saturday. Tone 4 [Dkh / ԴՁ]. John the Forerunner.

15. Sunday. Tone 4 [Dk / ԴԿ]. First [Sunday]. Dismissal Hymn [Hambartsi] Tone 4 [Dk / դկ]: "Glory to the Resurrection" (P'ark' harut'ean).

16. Monday. Tone 1 [Ah / ԱՁ]. Peter the Patriarch, Blaise, and the others.

17. Tuesday. Tone 1 [Ak / ԱԿ]. Anthony, and Tryphon, Barsama, and Onuphrius the hermits.

18. Wednesday. Tone 2 [Dz / ԲՁ]. Canticle of the Fathers of Repentance [Harst aghts.] Tone 1 [Ah / աձ]: "The youths sang" (Ergein mankunkn).

19. Thursday. Tone 2 [Bk / ԲԿ]. Theodosius the King, and the Holy Children of Ephesus.

21. Saturday. Tone 3 [Gk / ԳԿ]. Patriarchs Athanasius and Cyril.

22. Sunday. Tone 4 [Dz / ԴՁ]. Second [Sunday]. Canticle of the Fathers [Harst] Tone 2 [Bk / բկ]: "With voluntary" (Kamavorut'eamb). Dismissal Hymn [Hambartsi] Tone 4 [Dz / դձ]: "The resurrection of the Son of God" (Zharut'iun astuatsorduoyn).

23. Monday. Tone 4 [Dk / ԴԿ]. Cyriacus and Julitta, and Gordius, Polyeuctus, and Grigoris. Canticle of the Fathers [Harst] Tone 1 [Ak / ակ]: "Who art most high" (Or bardzreald es).

24. Tuesday. Tone 1 [Ah / ԱՁ]. Vahan of Goghtn, and Eugenie the Virgin, and the others. Canticle of the Fathers [Harst] Tone 3 [Gz / գձ]: "Without malice" (Anokhakal).

26. Thursday. Tone 2 [Dz / ԲՁ]. Eugenius, Macarius, and the others. Canticle of the Fathers [Harst] Tone 2 [Dz / բձ]: "With Thy saints" (End surbs k'o).

28. Saturday. Tone 3 [Gz / ԳՁ]. Gregory the Theologian.

29. Sunday. Tone 3 [Gk / ԳԿ]. Third [Sunday]. Canticle of the Fathers [Harst] Tone 3 [Gz / գձ]: "Creator of creatures" (Ararichd araratsots'). Dismissal Hymn [Hambartsi] Tone 3 [Gk / գկ]: "A vision like lightning" (Tesil p'aylaknadzev). Because for the songs written above following Transfiguration [Vardavar], see the First Volume, but for the Canticles of the Sundays and Fast days, and for the Tones, see here.

1. Thursday. Tone 8 [dk]. Ascension of Christ.
11. Sunday. Tone 2 [ak]. Coming of the Holy Spirit.

September
2. Saturday. Tone 5 [gz]. Of James and Simeon the Apostles. Read the scripture and the Gospel of the Tuesday of the Fast of Saint James.
3. Sunday. Tone 6 [gk]. Fourth [Sunday]. Canticle [Harts] Tone 3 [bz]: "We glorify Thee." Evening Hymn [Hambartsi] Tone 6 [gk]: "By the divine grave."

11. Monday. Tone 6 [gk]. Fast of the Holy Cross. Canticle [Harts] Tone 4 [bk]: "God of our fathers."
17. Sunday. Tone 4 [bk]. Exaltation of the Holy Cross. See in the first volume until the tenth Sunday. But for the Canticles [Harts] of Martyrs and Fasts, the Evening Hymns [Hambartsi] of the Resurrection, and the daily tones, see the second volume, verifying the month.

November
19. Sunday. Tone 3 [bdz]. Tenth [Sunday]. Evening Hymn [Hambartsi] Tone 3 [bz]: "The voice of good tidings." Pre-fast of the Advent [Yisnak] Fast.
20. Monday. Tone 4 [bk]. Fast of Advent [Yisnak]. Canticle [Harts] Tone 7 [dz]: "God of our fathers, who delivered."
21. Tuesday. Tone 5 [gz]. Of the Fast. Feast of the Presentation of the Holy Mother of God.
25. Saturday. Tone 1 [ahz]. Of Gregory and Nicholas the wonderworking Patriarchs, and of the other Nicholas the Patriarch.

THE SEVEN-DAY CYCLES OF THE ROMANS 4.

In January:

6. Tuesday. Tone 2 [BK - B-flat / Second Side]. Feast of the Nativity and Theophany of Christ our God. The intervening period of eating is forty-one days.

14. Wednesday. Tone 2 [BK - B-flat / Second Side]. Fast. Penitential Father [Harts] Tone 1 [A-dz - A / First Class]: "The youths sang in the furnace and said" (Yergeyin mankunkn i hnocin yev asyein).

15. Thursday. Tone 3 [GD - G / Third Class]. Feast of the Birth of John the Forerunner [Precursor].

17. Saturday. Tone 4 [D-dz - D / Fourth Class]. [Feast of] Peter the Patriarch, Blaise the Bishop, and Abisalom the Deacon.

18. Sunday. Tone 4 [DK - D-flat / Fourth Side]. First [Sunday]. Hambartsi Tone 4 [DK - D-flat / Fourth Side]: "Glory to Your resurrection, O Lord" (P'ark' harut'yan k'o ter); "Having taken upon Yourself the cross" (Khach'il i yandzn) to the end.

19. Monday. Tone 1 [AD - A / First Class]. [Feast of] Anton the Anchorite.

20. Tuesday. Tone 1 [AK - A-flat / First Side]. [Feast of] Theodosius the King and the Holy Babes of Ephesus.

21. Wednesday. Tone 2 [BD - B / Second Class]. Penitential Father [Harts] Tone 1 [AK - A-flat / First Side]: "Glorified forever is the God of our fathers" (P'aravoryal havytyan astvats harts'n merots').

22. Thursday. Tone 2 [BK - B-flat / Second Side]. [Feast of] Cyriacus and his mother Julitta, and of the witnesses Gordius, Polyeuctus, and Gregory. Father [Harts] Tone 1 [A-dz - A / First Class]: "You, the righteous and upright judge" (Uzk'ez ardar yev ughigh da...).

24. Saturday. Tone 3 [GK - G-flat / Third Side]. [Feast of] Patriarchs Athanasius and Cyril, and Gregory the Theologian.

25. Sunday. Tone 4 [DD - D / Fourth Class]. Second [Sunday]. Father [Harts] Tone 2 [BK - B-flat / Second Side]: "By Your voluntary will You came to suffering" (Kamavorut'yamb yekir i ch'arch'arans). Hambartsi Tone 4 [DD - D / Fourth Class]: "The archangel [announced] the resurrection of the Son of God" (Zyarut'yun astvatsordvoyn hreshtakapetn).

26. Monday. Tone 4 [DK - D-flat / Fourth Side]. [Feast of] Vahan of Goghtn, and Eugenia the Virgin, and her father Philip, and her mother Claudia, and the two brothers Sergius and Apiton, and the two eunuchs Protus and Hyacinthus. Father [Harts] Tone 1 [AK - A-flat / First Side]: "Holy God, Creator of eternity" (Surb astvats ararich' yavityenits').

27. Tuesday. Tone 1 [AD - A / First Class]. [Feast of] Tryphon, Barsamas, and Onuphrius the Anchorites.

28. Wednesday. Tone 1 [AK - A-flat / First Side]. Penitential Father [Harts] Tone 2 [B-dz - B / Second Class]: "Who saved the three youths from the furnace" (Vor abrets'uts'er zeris mankunsn i hnoc'inn).

29. Thursday. Tone 2 [BD - B / Second Class]. [Feast of] Eugenius, Macarius, Valerius, Candidus, and Aquilas. Father [Harts] Tone 1 [AK - A-flat / First Side]: "Who are exalted and rest in the saints" (Vor bardzryald es yev i surbs hanguts'yal).

31. Saturday. Tone 3 [GD - G / Third Class]. [Feast of] Thaddeus the Apostle and Sandukht the Virgin. For the Apostle Thaddeus, start from the order set down below in the First Volume after Vardavar; but the Father [Harts] hymns and the tones are to be taken here.

In February:

1. Sunday. Tone 3 [GK - G-flat / Third Side]. Third [Sunday]. Father [Harts] Tone 3 [GD - G / Third Class]: "Creator of all creation, O Christ, today" (Ararich'd araradsots' K'ristos aysor). Hambartsi Tone 3 [GK - G-flat / Third Side]: "The sight of the lightning-like angel on the stone" (Tesil p'aylaknadzev zvart'noyn i vimin).

2. Monday. Tone 4 [DD - D / Fourth Class]. [Feast of] Cyprian the Bishop and the forty-five martyrs, and of the virgins Justina, Euphemia, and Christina. Father [Harts] Tone 2 [BD - B / Second Class]: "You who do not harbor resentment and are most merciful, do not scatter us" (Anokhakal yev baregut' mi ts'rer).

3. Tuesday. Tone 4 [DK - D-flat / Fourth Side]. [Feast of] Athenogenes the Bishop, and his ten disciples, and the five martyrs. Father [Harts] Tone 2 [BD - B / Second Class]: "With Your saints we praise You" (Und surbs k'o barebanemk' uzk'ez).

5. Thursday. Tone 1 [AK]. Of the Holy Forefathers: Adam, Abel, Seth, Enosh, Enoch, Noah, Melchizedek, Abraham, Isaac, Jacob, Joseph, Moses, Aaron, Eleazar, Joshua, Samuel, Samson, Jephthah, Barak, Gideon, and the other holy forefathers.
7. Saturday. Tone 4 [BK]. Of the sons and grandsons of Saint Gregory the Illuminator: Aristakes, Vrtanes, Husik, Grigoris, and Daniel.
8. Sunday. Tone 5 [GC]. Fourth [Sunday after Theophany]. Canticle of the Fathers, Tone 6 [GK]: "Today Christ is sacrificed for us" (Aysor zenav mez patarag). Dismissal Hymn [Hambartsi], Tone 7 [GY]: "With the awesome voice of resurrection" (I dzayn ahegh yarutean) [including "The angel"].
9. Monday. Tone 6 [GK]. Of the Maccabees: Eleazar the priest, Shamuna, and her seven sons. Canticle of the Fathers, Tone 4 [BK]: "We bless You" (Orhnemk zkez).
10. Tuesday. Tone 5 [GC]. Of the twelve prophets: Hosea, Amos, Micah, Joel, Obadiah, Jonah, Nahum, Habakkuk, Zephaniah, Haggai, Zechariah, and Malachi.
11. Wednesday. Tone 8 [DK]. Canticle of the Fathers of repentance, Tone 4 [BK]: "Blessed art You, Lord, God of our fathers" (Harts-n merots orhneal es ter).
12. Thursday. Tone 1 [AD]. Of Sophia and her three daughters: Pistis, Elpis, and Agape. Canticle of the Fathers, Tone 4 [BK]: "Those who triumphed in the battle of the law" (Ork i marti norinats-n yaghtets-in).
14. Saturday. Tone 3 [BD]. Presentation of our Lord to the Temple [Tiarnndaraj].
15. Sunday. Tone 4 [BK]. Fifth [Sunday]. Dismissal Hymn [Hambartsi], Tone 4 [BK]: "The resurrection of Christ" (Zyarutiun Kriskosi). Eve of the Fast of Catechumens [Arachavorats].
16. Monday. Tone 5 [GD]. Fast of Catechumens [Arachavorats]. Canticle of the Fathers of repentance, Tone 4 [BK]: "Lord God of our fathers" (Ter astuats harts-n merots).
22. Sunday. Tone 3 [BD]. Dismissal Hymn [Hambartsi], Tone 3 [BD]: "The marvelous women" (Skancheli kanayk-n).
28. Saturday. Tone 1 [AD]. Of Sahak the Parthian. From here onward, look under letter 'P' [Փ] in the calendar directory, for the letter 'K' [Ք] directory is only for leap years and is used until the end of February.
29. Sunday. Tone 2 [AK]. Mid-Fast. Canticle of the Fathers, Tone 8 [DK]: "Who sittest upon the awesome throne" (Or yaheghakerp ator nstis). Dismissal Hymn [Hambartsi], Tone 2 [AK]: "We shall sing to You with the angels" (Yergests-uk kez end hresh...).
7. Sunday. Tone 8 [DK]. Great Eve of Lent [Bun Barekendan].

## page_0640_auto

CONCERNING THE FORTHCOMING TABLE-FORM CYCLE

The table-form cycle, which is arranged on the following page, is the perpetual cycle of the Great Paschal Cycle [Great Era / 500-year cycle] containing 532 years, which by means of the year of Christ unerringly indicates the Dominical Letter [Letter of the Year / Taregir] and the Epact [Veradir] perpetually. For upon the completion of the 532 years, it begins again from the start, only changing the date. At the beginning of the lines, the placed index numbers are the years of Christ; the topmost line contains the Epacts, and those placed in the boxes are the Dominical Letters. 

See, therefore, how it begins: it starts at the year 1909, which is placed at the beginning of the first line. Going from that year to the first box, you will find the Dominical Letter of that same year, Ը [8], and proceeding directly upward from that Dominical Letter Ը, you will find the Epact of the same year, ԺԹ [19]. And on arriving at the year 1910, going forward one box from the Dominical Letter Ը, in the same line you will find there the Dominical Letter of the year 1910, Ռ [R]. And proceeding directly upward from that Dominical Letter, you will find placed there the Epact of the same year, Լ [30]. And by this order, as much as the year increases, you go forward one box in the same line, where you will find the Dominical Letter of the year, and directly upward from the Dominical Letter, the Epact. And as you proceed in this orderly way until you reach the last box of the same line, in which your year will be 1927, you will find in the same box the Dominical Letter of that same year, Յ [Y], and directly at the head of it, the Epact Ը [8]. Then, at the end of that same year, the first line also ends, which has 19 years.

Then you must return to the beginning of the second line, in which you will find the year 1928, and opposite to it in the first box, the Dominical Letter ԽԼ [XL], and directly at the head of it, the Epact ԺԹ [19]. Keep this same instruction, which was spoken concerning the first line, in this line, and in the third, and in the fourth, and in all other lines until the end.

CONCERNING THE TABLE-FORM CYCLE

By this order, on reaching the last line, in which is the year 2422, and in its last box, in which is the Dominical Letter ՈՉ [OCh], and the year is 2440, the Great Paschal Cycle is completed, which is 532 years. For one page of your Table has 28 lines according to the cycle of the Solar Cycle [Seven-literals / Eotneryak], and each line lasts 19 years, according to the number of the 19 Epacts; and 19 times 28, or 28 times 19, is 532, which is one cycle.

For that reason, you must of necessity return here to the same first beginning line according to the rule of the cycle, and begin anew; in which your year will be 2441, and opposite to it in the first box is placed the Dominical Letter Ը [8], and at the head, the Epact ԺԹ [19], according to the first order; for you must only change the year, and proceed in an orderly way similar to the first.

But if you should wish to find the Dominical Letter of past years, you must add to the year you have, once or twice, 532 years, which is one or two cycles, and find in the forthcoming table your summed year, and the Dominical Letter of that same year is the Dominical Letter of the past year which you are seeking.

For this reason we placed the index of the cycle with this Directory [Tonatsuyts], so that having the year of whatever year at hand, by means of it you may find the Letter of the Year; and then, having found the Canon [Khoran] of the same Dominical Letter in the Second Volume, you may conduct it therewith without labor and perpetually.

Although the Epacts were not as necessary as the Dominical Letters, yet we placed them for the sake of bringing the cycle into harmony. As for the Seven-literals [Dominical Letters / Eotneryak], they are placed in the Canon of each Dominical Letter.

## page_0642_human

COLOPHON
OF THIS FOURTH JERUSALEM EDITION

By the grace of the Lord, this fourth publication of the Book of Hours [Zhamagirk] and Directory [Tonatsuyts] of the Armenian Church has been brought to a successful completion, in a dual printing of the Chancel [Ateni] and Hand [Dzerats] volumes.

Those things which have been in common use in all Orthodox churches of the Armenians since the days of Lord Simeon of Yerevan, the ever-memorable and highly meritorious Catholicos of All Armenians, have been kept unchanged.

We deemed it appropriate only to introduce certain changes in letters and words, or in the form of instructions and explanations, as they suited accepted customs and ease of understanding.

Herein, we have replaced the Armenian letter-numbers in the excerpts of the simple calendar and in the Five-Hundred-Year table with accepted [Arabic] numerals.

In the references of the readings, we removed the Euthalian chapters, and we placed the numbers of the chapters and verses before the words of the commencements and endings, which we also abbreviated; for example, instead of writing "Gospel according to Luke, chapter 22, (Merձetsav) 'The feast of unleavened bread drew near' to 'the twelve tribes of Israel', verse 30," we wrote simply: "Luke 22:1 (Merձetsav) to verse 30 (Israel)."

We corrected the spelling of names, and we put Lucian instead of Lunkianos, Adrian instead of Andrianos, Pelagia instead of Peligia, Tarachus instead of Taragros, Eugraphius instead of Grabos, and so forth.

## page_0643_human

In the indexes of the calendar of the second volume of the Tonatsuyts [Directory of Feasts], we followed a loose simplicity, preferring it
to the crowded layout of tables and columns. And
where the feasts of the saints were combined, we separated them
with additions.
At the end of the Manual of Hours [Book of Hours], we newly prepared
the succession of Catholicoi, following the newest
studies, and we placed the same in the place of the incomplete
list of the previous printings.
And whatever may still be deficient in these words, we commit
to the care of those who shall come after us.
In Jerusalem, Apostolic See of Saint James,
August 15, ՌՅԿԴ [1364 Armenian Era / AD 1915]...
